# NYC What To Do

**Rolling window:** 2026-09-22 through 2026-09-28

## Best date plans

### 2026-09-28 — Brooklyn Book Festival Bookend closing night + Sawa

**Brooklyn Book Festival Bookend closing night, then dinner.** Lebanese at Sawa. It is a 21-minute walk from the venue. It starts at 6:30 pm, so eat afterwards.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=Brooklyn%2C+NY&destination=75+5th+Avenue%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11217&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://brooklynbookfestival.org/) · [Restaurant](https://www.sawabk.com/)

### 2026-09-23 — Rana Fifteen + Brooklyn Book Festival Bookend Events

**Dinner, then Brooklyn Book Festival Bookend Events.** Turkish at Rana Fifteen. It is a 22-minute walk from the venue. Brooklyn Book Festival Bookend Events starts at 7 pm, so book the table early.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=209+4th+Avenue%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11217&destination=Brooklyn%2C+NY&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://brooklynbookfestival.org/) · [Restaurant](https://www.ranafifteen.com/)

### 2026-09-27 — Brooklyn Book Festival Festival Day and Literary Marketplace + Sawa

**Brooklyn Book Festival Festival Day and Literary Marketplace, then a meal.** Lebanese at Sawa. It is a 21-minute walk from the venue. It starts at 10 am, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=209+Joralemon+St%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11201&destination=75+5th+Avenue%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11217&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://brooklynbookfestival.org/) · [Restaurant](https://www.sawabk.com/)

### 2026-09-25 — Robin Wall Kimmerer at LUNGS Harvest Arts Festival + Thai Diner

**Robin Wall Kimmerer at LUNGS Harvest Arts Festival, then a meal.** Thai American at Thai Diner. It is a 19-minute walk from the venue. It starts at 2 pm, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=710+East+5th+Street%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10009&destination=186+Mott+Street%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10012&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://lungsnyc.org/harvest-arts-festival/) · [Restaurant](https://www.thaidiner.com/)

### 2026-09-22 — Falansai + Alabaster DePlume, Tara Lily

**Dinner, then Alabaster DePlume, Tara Lily.** Vietnamese American at Falansai. It is a 5-minute walk from the venue. Alabaster DePlume, Tara Lily starts at 7 pm, so book the table early.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=112+Harrison+Place%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11237&destination=599+Johnson+Avenue%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11237&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.elsewhere.club/events/1992079565606) · [Event booking](https://www.eventbrite.com/e/alabaster-deplume-tara-lily-tickets-1992079565606) · [Restaurant](https://www.falansai.com/)

### 2026-09-27 — Clio Art Fair final day + Yellow Rose

**Clio Art Fair final day, then a meal.** Tex-Mex at Yellow Rose. It is 1.5 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. It starts at 12 pm, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=511+W+25th+St%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10001&destination=102+3rd+Avenue%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10003&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.clioartfair.com/visitor-info-sept-2026) · [Restaurant](https://www.yellowrosenyc.com/)

### 2026-09-26 — Salon Zürcher: 100 Women of Spirit + + Thai Diner

**Salon Zürcher: 100 Women of Spirit +, then dinner.** Thai American at Thai Diner. It is a 9-minute walk from the venue. It starts at 5 pm, so eat afterwards.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=33+Bleecker+St%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10012&destination=186+Mott+Street%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10012&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.galeriezurcher.com) · [Restaurant](https://www.thaidiner.com/)

### 2026-09-27 — Smorgasburg Prospect Park + Gertrude's

**Smorgasburg Prospect Park, then a meal.** Jewish American bistro at Gertrude's. It is a 24-minute walk from the venue. It starts at 11 am, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=Breeze+Hill%2C+Prospect+Park+%28enter+at+Lincoln+Road%29%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY&destination=605+Carlton+Avenue%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11238&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.smorgasburg.com/) · [Restaurant](https://www.gertrudesbrooklyn.com/)

#### [Paper Tiger Opening Night](https://www.filmlinc.org/films/paper-tiger/)

James Gray's Paper Tiger opens the 64th New York Film Festival, starring Adam Driver, Miles Teller, and Scarlett Johansson.
**Timing:** Opening Night listing shows 6:00pm Q&A plus additional evening screenings.

## Where to eat

Factual pass kept the restaurant/menu items and dated food events that have usable public support, corrected risky prices, dates, addresses, classifications and duplicates, and rejected only duplicate cards or unsupported “new opening” framing where necessary.

Dining research: 2026-09-22T10:34:35+00:00

No Michelin-star claims are added or retained. Rezdora’s restaurant-site Michelin imagery was not treated as independent award evidence; no direct Michelin Guide source was needed because the edited cards do not make Michelin-star or Michelin-selection claims.

### Manhatta three- or four-course dinner set menu

Special menus — Manhatta

Official menu lists a structured skyline dinner: three courses or four courses, with optional wine pairings.

Price: Three Courses | 120; Wine Pairing with Three-Course Menu | 75; Four Courses | 148; Wine Pairing with Four-Course Menu | 95  
Schedule: dinner; exact days/hours not stated on fetched menu chunk  
Conditions: Official Manhatta menu lists dinner at Three Courses | 120 and Four Courses | 148, with wine pairings at 75/95 and butter-poached lobster +24; tax, tip and service terms were not exposed. Source: https://www.manhattarestaurant.com/menus/  
[Source](https://www.manhattarestaurant.com/menus/)

### Crown Shy lunch prix fixe availability

Special menus — Crown Shy

Official lunch page explicitly states a prix fixe menu is available alongside lunch dishes such as pork katsu sandwich, salmon, chicken kofta, and flatbreads.

Price: prix fixe price unknown; a la carte lunch items checked at $8-$49  
Schedule: lunch; exact days/hours not stated in fetched menu chunk  
Conditions: Official lunch menu says “Prix Fixe Menu Available,” but the captured page exposes only a la carte lunch prices ($8-$49) and does not expose the prix-fixe price, course count, days, tax, tip or service terms. Source: https://www.crownshy.nyc/menus/  
[Source](https://www.crownshy.nyc/menus/)

### Rezdora Parmigiano Reggiano tasting

Special menus — Rezdora

Official dinner menu includes a focused Parmigiano Reggiano tasting described as a journey through five ages of Parmigiano, a concise special-format add-on for cheese/pasta-focused dining.

Price: $26  
Schedule: dinner menu; exact days/hours not stated in fetched menu chunk  
Conditions: Official dinner menu lists “Parmigiano Reggiano Tasting” at $26, described as “A journey through 5 ages of Parmigiano”; it is a regular menu item, not a dated event. Source: https://www.rezdora.nyc/menus/

### Queens Night Market fall Saturdays through October 31

Pop-ups — Queens Night Market

Official FAQ lists the market as open Saturdays with a finite season through October 31, making it a good affordable Queens food-market option with rotating vendors and many cuisines.

Price: Free admission; vendor prices vary. Adjacent parking listed as $15 for cars during market hours, subject to change.  
Schedule: Saturdays 4:00 PM-midnight through 2026-10-31; rain or shine except unsafe weather.  
Conditions: Official FAQ says Saturdays 4 p.m.-midnight from April 18 through October 31, with a US Open break August 23-September 18, rain or shine except unsafe weather. FAQ does not print the year in the season line; checked for the 2026 guide date, with header showing next market Sep 26. Admission is free for regular markets; FAQ’s sneak-preview note still references 2025. Source: https://queensnightmarket.com/faq  
From: 2026-04-18  
Through: 2026-10-31  
[Source](https://queensnightmarket.com/faq)
